Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Rayleigh-Ritz投影的动态图特征向量更新算法，用于处理图结构变化时邻接矩阵或拉普拉斯矩阵特征向量的高效更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征分解方法适用于静态矩阵，但无法有效处理动态变化的图结构（节点/边的增删）。现实应用中图数据频繁变化，需要能够高效更新特征向量的算法。

Method: 基于Rayleigh-Ritz投影框架，将原始特征值问题投影到受限子空间。利用特征向量扰动分析思想构建投影子空间，该子空间理想情况下包含目标特征向量对应的不变子空间。

Result: 相比竞争方法，该框架具有更低的计算和内存复杂度。实验结果显示在特征向量近似和下游学习任务（中心节点识别和节点聚类）方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效处理动态图特征向量更新的新算法框架，在计算效率和实际应用性能方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Several graph data mining, signal processing, and machine learning downstream tasks rely on information related to the eigenvectors of the associated adjacency or Laplacian matrix. Classical eigendecomposition methods are powerful when the matrix remains static but cannot be applied to problems where the matrix entries are updated or the number of rows and columns increases frequently. Such scenarios occur routinely in graph analytics when the graph is changing dynamically and either edges and/or nodes are being added and removed. This paper puts forth a new algorithmic framework to update the eigenvectors associated with the leading eigenvalues of an initial adjacency or Laplacian matrix as the graph evolves dynamically. The proposed algorithm is based on Rayleigh-Ritz projections, in which the original eigenvalue problem is projected onto a restricted subspace which ideally encapsulates the invariant subspace associated with the sought eigenvectors. Following ideas from eigenvector perturbation analysis, we present a new methodology to build the projection subspace. The proposed framework features lower computational and memory complexity with respect to competitive alternatives while empirical results show strong qualitative performance, both in terms of eigenvector approximation and accuracy of downstream learning tasks of central node identification and node clustering.

</details>


### [116] [On the role of memorization in learned priors for geophysical inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19629)
*Ali Siahkoohi,Davide Sabeddu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文探讨了基于深度生成模型的学习先验在地震反演中的应用，指出由于地质数据稀缺，模型容易记忆训练样本而非学习真实地质分布，并推导了记忆化先验下的后验分布形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质应用中代表性地下模型数据集稀缺，导致基于深度生成模型的学习先验容易记忆训练样本而非学习真实地质分布，这限制了其在反演问题中的有效性。

Method: 分析记忆化先验的数学形式：对于扩散模型，记忆化产生闭式高斯混合先验；通过线性化前向算子，得到由局部雅可比矩阵控制宽度和偏移的高斯混合后验分布。

Result: 在理想化反演问题上验证了理论预测，并通过全波形反演中的扩散后验采样展示了记忆化的实际后果。

Conclusion: 深度生成模型在地质反演中面临记忆化风险，需要开发更鲁棒的训练方法以避免对有限训练数据的过拟合，确保学习到真实的地质分布。

Abstract: Learned priors based on deep generative models offer data-driven regularization for seismic inversion, but training them requires a dataset of representative subsurface models -- a resource that is inherently scarce in geoscience applications. Since the training objective of most generative models can be cast as maximum likelihood on a finite dataset, any such model risks converging to the empirical distribution -- effectively memorizing the training examples rather than learning the underlying geological distribution. We show that the posterior under such a memorized prior reduces to a reweighted empirical distribution -- i.e., a likelihood-weighted lookup among the stored training examples. For diffusion models specifically, memorization yields a Gaussian mixture prior in closed form, and linearizing the forward operator around each training example gives a Gaussian mixture posterior whose components have widths and shifts governed by the local Jacobian. We validate these predictions on a stylized inverse problem and demonstrate the consequences of memorization through diffusion posterior sampling for full waveform inversion.

</details>


### [117] [Model Selection and Parameter Estimation of Multi-dimensional Gaussian Mixture Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19657)
*Xinyu Liu,Hai Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于学习多维高斯混合模型（GMM）的傅里叶基算法框架，包括模型阶数选择、参数估计和维度约简，在样本复杂度和计算效率上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯混合模型学习方法在模型阶数选择、计算效率和样本复杂度方面存在局限性，特别是在高维场景下。需要开发能够同时实现最优模型选择、高效参数估计和维度约简的统一框架。

Method: 1. 建立模型选择的信息论下界：证明区分k分量混合与简单模型需要Ω(Δ^{-(4k-4)})样本量。2. 提出基于阈值的模型阶数估计器：通过随机傅里叶测量向量构建经验协方差矩阵，评估其谱间隙。3. 基于梯度的参数估计：采用数据驱动的分数初始化策略，保证快速收敛。4. 高维场景集成PCA进行维度约简。

Result: 1. 模型选择算法的时间复杂度为O(k²n)，与样本量线性相关。2. 样本复杂度与理论下界匹配，证明在分量分离距离Δ方面具有极小极大最优性。3. 参数估计达到最优参数收敛率O_p(n^{-1/2})。4. 数值实验显示在估计精度和计算时间上优于传统EM方法。

Conclusion: 提出的傅里叶基算法框架为多维高斯混合模型学习提供了统一的解决方案，在模型选择、参数估计和计算效率方面均达到理论最优，特别适用于高维场景，为实际应用提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of learning multi-dimensional Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs), with a specific focus on model order selection and efficient mixing distribution estimation. We first establish an information-theoretic lower bound on the critical sample complexity required for reliable model selection. More specifically, we show that distinguishing a $k$-component mixture from a simpler model necessitates a sample size scaling of $Ω(Δ^{-(4k-4)})$. We then propose a thresholding-based estimation algorithm that evaluates the spectral gap of an empirical covariance matrix constructed from random Fourier measurement vectors. This parameter-free estimator operates with an efficient time complexity of $\mathcal{O}(k^2 n)$, scaling linearly with the sample size. We demonstrate that the sample complexity of our method matches the established lower bound, confirming its minimax optimality with respect to the component separation distance $Δ$.
  Conditioned on the estimated model order, we subsequently introduce a gradient-based minimization method for parameter estimation. To effectively navigate the non-convex objective landscape, we employ a data-driven, score-based initialization strategy that guarantees rapid convergence. We prove that this method achieves the optimal parametric convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}_p(n^{-1/2})$ for estimating the component means. To enhance the algorithm's efficiency in high-dimensional regimes where the ambient dimension exceeds the number of mixture components (i.e., \(d > k\)), we integrate principal component analysis (PCA) for dimension reduction. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our Fourier-based algorithmic framework outperforms conventional Expectation-Maximization (EM) methods in both estimation accuracy and computational time.

</details>


### [118] [A two-step sequential approach for hyperparameter selection in finite context models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19736)
*José Contente,Ana Martins,Armando J. Pinho,Sónia Gouveia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种用于有限上下文模型超参数选择的两步顺序方法，通过分解联合优化问题，先使用序列依赖度量估计上下文长度k，再通过最大似然估计平滑参数α，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限上下文模型在压缩符号序列时，超参数选择通常通过穷举搜索完成，计算成本高且随模型复杂度扩展性差，需要更高效的超参数选择方法。

Method: 提出两步顺序方法：1) 使用分类序列依赖度量（Cramér's ν、Cohen's κ和部分互信息）估计上下文长度k；2) 在选定k的条件下，通过最大似然估计平滑参数α。

Result: 模拟实验显示依赖度量对k的变化比α更敏感，支持顺序估计策略；随着样本量增加，超参数估计精度提高；该方法在平均比特率方面与穷举网格搜索性能相当，但计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 提出的顺序方法是有限上下文模型中穷举超参数调优的实用且计算高效的替代方案，在模拟数据上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Finite-context models (FCMs) are widely used for compressing symbolic sequences such as DNA, where predictive performance depends critically on the context length k and smoothing parameter α. In practice, these hyperparameters are typically selected through exhaustive search, which is computationally expensive and scales poorly with model complexity. This paper proposes a statistically grounded two-step sequential approach for efficient hyperparameter selection in FCMs. The key idea is to decompose the joint optimization problem into two independent stages. First, the context length k is estimated using categorical serial dependence measures, including Cramér's ν, Cohen's \k{appa} and partial mutual information (pami). Second, the smoothing parameter α is estimated via maximum likelihood conditional on the selected context length k. Simulation experiments were conducted on synthetic symbolic sequences generated by FCMs across multiple (k, α) configurations, considering a four-letter alphabet and different sample sizes. Results show that the dependence measures are substantially more sensitive to variations in k than in α, supporting the sequential estimation strategy. As expected, the accuracy of the hyperparameter estimation improves with increasing sample size. Furthermore, the proposed method achieves compression performance comparable to exhaustive grid search in terms of average bitrate (bits per symbol), while substantially reducing computational cost. Overall, the results on simulated data show that the proposed sequential approach is a practical and computationally efficient alternative to exhaustive hyperparameter tuning in FCMs.

</details>


### [119] [Explainable cluster analysis: a bagging approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19840)
*Federico Maria Quetti,Elena Ballante,Silvia Figini,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于集成学习的聚类框架，通过bagging和特征dropout生成特征重要性评分，提高聚类可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法缺乏可解释性，无法揭示哪些特征驱动了观测值的分组，限制了聚类结果的实际应用价值

Method: 集成聚类框架结合bagging和特征dropout，采用信息论方法计算每个特征与聚类标签的互信息，并通过聚类有效性度量加权，最终聚合得到特征重要性评分

Result: 方法在多个模拟和真实数据集上验证有效，能够同时输出共识聚类划分和特征重要性度量，提升聚类稳定性和可解释性

Conclusion: 提出的集成聚类框架成功解决了聚类可解释性问题，通过特征重要性评分机制使聚类结果更易于理解和解释，特别适用于小样本或噪声环境

Abstract: A major limitation of clustering approaches is their lack of explainability: methods rarely provide insight into which features drive the grouping of similar observations. To address this limitation, we propose an ensemble-based clustering framework that integrates bagging and feature dropout to generate feature importance scores, in analogy with feature importance mechanisms in supervised random forests. By leveraging multiple bootstrap resampling schemes and aggregating the resulting partitions, the method improves stability and robustness of the cluster definition, particularly in small-sample or noisy settings. Feature importance is assessed through an information-theoretic approach: at each step, the mutual information between each feature and the estimated cluster labels is computed and weighted by a measure of clustering validity to emphasize well-formed partitions, before being aggregated into a final score. The method outputs both a consensus partition and a corresponding measure of feature importance, enabling a unified interpretation of clustering structure and variable relevance. Its effectiveness is demonstrated on multiple simulated and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [120] [Minimax Generalized Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19874)
*Kartheek Bondugula,Santiago Mazuelas,Aritz Pérez,Anqi Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出最小最大广义交叉熵（MGCE），通过凸优化解决现有广义交叉熵（GCE）的非凸优化问题，在标签噪声下实现更好的准确率、收敛速度和校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义交叉熵（GCE）在交叉熵（CE）和平均绝对误差（MAE）之间提供权衡，但其非凸优化容易导致欠拟合，在复杂数据集上表现不佳。需要一种既能保持鲁棒性又能避免优化困难的损失函数。

Method: 提出最小最大广义交叉熵（MGCE），采用双层凸优化框架，通过隐式微分计算随机梯度，实现分类边界的凸优化。该方法能提供分类误差的上界。

Result: 在基准数据集上，MGCE在标签噪声存在时表现出更强的准确性、更快的收敛速度和更好的校准性能，相比现有方法有明显优势。

Conclusion: MGCE通过凸优化解决了GCE的非凸优化问题，在保持鲁棒性的同时避免了欠拟合，为监督分类提供了更有效的损失函数选择，特别适用于标签噪声场景。

Abstract: Loss functions play a central role in supervised classification. Cross-entropy (CE) is widely used, whereas the mean absolute error (MAE) loss can offer robustness but is difficult to optimize. Interpolating between the CE and MAE losses, generalized cross-entropy (GCE) has recently been introduced to provide a trade-off between optimization difficulty and robustness. Existing formulations of GCE result in a non-convex optimization over classification margins that is prone to underfitting, leading to poor performances with complex datasets. In this paper, we propose a minimax formulation of generalized cross-entropy (MGCE) that results in a convex optimization over classification margins. Moreover, we show that MGCEs can provide an upper bound on the classification error. The proposed bilevel convex optimization can be efficiently implemented using stochastic gradient computed via implicit differentiation. Using benchmark datasets, we show that MGCE achieves strong accuracy, faster convergence, and better calibration, especially in the presence of label noise.

</details>


### [121] [Deep Autocorrelation Modeling for Time-Series Forecasting: Progress and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19899)
*Hao Wang,Licheng Pan,Qingsong Wen,Jialin Yu,Zhichao Chen,Chunyuan Zheng,Xiaoxi Li,Zhixuan Chu,Chao Xu,Mingming Gong,Haoxuan Li,Yuan Lu,Zhouchen Lin,Philip Torr,Yan Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文对深度时间序列预测中的自相关建模进行了系统性综述，提出了涵盖模型架构和学习目标的新分类法，并从自相关视角分析了该领域的发展脉络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自相关是时间序列数据的固有特性，在深度时间序列预测中，自相关同时存在于输入历史和标签序列中，带来了两个核心挑战：设计能建模历史序列自相关的神经架构，以及设计能建模标签序列自相关的学习目标。现有研究虽已取得进展，但缺乏从自相关角度对这两个方面进行系统性综述的研究。

Method: 论文采用系统性文献综述方法，从自相关建模的视角对深度时间序列预测领域进行全面梳理。提出了一个新颖的分类法，该分类法同时涵盖模型架构和学习目标两个方面，而现有综述往往忽视或不足够讨论后者。通过统一的自相关中心视角，深入分析了相关文献的动机、见解和发展脉络。

Result: 论文提供了深度时间序列预测中自相关建模的全面综述，建立了新的分类框架，系统性地分析了模型架构和学习目标两个维度的发展。创建了包含相关论文和资源的完整列表，可在GitHub仓库中获取。

Conclusion: 该综述填补了深度时间序列预测领域从自相关角度进行系统性分析的空白，为研究者提供了统一的视角来理解该领域的发展历程和未来方向。提出的分类法和分析框架有助于推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Autocorrelation is a defining characteristic of time-series data, where each observation is statistically dependent on its predecessors. In the context of deep time-series forecasting, autocorrelation arises in both the input history and the label sequences, presenting two central research challenges: (1) designing neural architectures that model autocorrelation in history sequences, and (2) devising learning objectives that model autocorrelation in label sequences. Recent studies have made strides in tackling these challenges, but a systematic survey examining both aspects remains lacking. To bridge this gap, this paper provides a comprehensive review of deep time-series forecasting from the perspective of autocorrelation modeling. In contrast to existing surveys, this work makes two distinctive contributions. First, it proposes a novel taxonomy that encompasses recent literature on both model architectures and learning objectives -- whereas prior surveys neglect or inadequately discuss the latter aspect. Second, it offers a thorough analysis of the motivations, insights, and progression of the surveyed literature from a unified, autocorrelation-centric perspective, providing a holistic overview of the evolution of deep time-series forecasting. The full list of papers and resources is available at https://github.com/Master-PLC/Awesome-TSF-Papers.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Doubly-Unlinked Regression for Dependent Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19506)
*Anik Burman,Sayantan Choudhury,Debangan Dey*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出双重未链接回归模型，同时处理协变量-响应配对丢失和响应-域配对丢失，开发REPAIR变分贝叶斯方法进行推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在依赖数据问题中，不仅协变量与响应之间的对应关系可能丢失，响应与诱导依赖结构的潜在时间、空间或几何域之间的对应关系也可能丢失。现有研究缺乏对这种双重对应关系丢失情况的系统处理。

Method: 提出双重未链接回归模型，包含两个潜在置换矩阵分别表示协变量-响应链接和响应-域链接的未知性。开发REPAIR（基于块结构置换模型的变分贝叶斯方法），通过捕捉局部扰乱来降低计算复杂度。

Result: 表征了回归参数和潜在置换恢复的信噪比机制，证明回归系数的相合估计可以在比精确置换恢复更弱的条件下实现。仿真和应用示例验证了REPAIR的实证表现并支持理论结果。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统处理了依赖数据中双重对应关系丢失的问题，统一了混洗回归和潜在域置换模型，为这类问题提供了理论分析和实用的推理方法。

Abstract: Shuffled regression concerns settings in which covariates and responses are observed without their correct pairing. In dependent-data problems, a second form of missing correspondence can arise when responses are also detached from the latent temporal, spatial, or geometric domain that induces their dependence structure. We study regression under this joint loss of correspondence and, to our knowledge, provide the first systematic treatment of this setting. Specifically, we consider a doubly-unlinked regression model in which both the covariate-response link and the response-domain link are unknown, represented by two latent permutation matrices, while dependence is induced by an unobserved stochastic process. This framework unifies shuffled regression and latent-domain permutation models within a common dependent-data setting. We characterize signal-to-noise regimes governing recovery of the regression parameter and the latent permutations, and show that consistent estimation of the regression coefficient can be achieved under strictly weaker conditions than exact permutation recovery. To address the combinatorial difficulty of inference, we develop REPAIR, a variational Bayes method based on a block-structured permutation model that captures localized scrambling while substantially reducing computational complexity. Simulations and an applied example illustrate the empirical behavior of REPAIR and support the theoretical results.

</details>


### [123] [Minimax and Adaptive Covariance Matrix Estimation under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19703)
*T. Tony Cai,Yicheng Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高维带状协方差矩阵在差分隐私约束下的极小极大和自适应估计，提出了新的隐私保护块状三对角估计器，并建立了隐私van Trees不等式来证明最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵在高维数据分析中具有基础性作用。在差分隐私约束下，需要研究高维带状协方差矩阵的估计问题，因为隐私保护会引入额外的误差成本，这种误差与维度呈多项式依赖关系，而非隐私设置下的误差通常与维度无关。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的差分隐私块状三对角估计器；开发了新的差分隐私van Trees不等式来建立最优性，并构造了精心设计的先验分布以获得匹配的极小极大下界；进一步引入了基于分层三对角方法的自适应估计器，无需先验知识即可达到最优速率。

Result: 提出的隐私保护估计器在算子范数和Frobenius范数下均实现了极小极大最优收敛速率；隐私引起的误差表现出与维度相关的多项式依赖关系，揭示了隐私保护的显著额外成本；自适应估计器在无需先验知识的情况下达到了最优速率（最多相差对数因子）；数值实验验证了理论结果并说明了隐私-准确性权衡。

Conclusion: 该论文为高维带状协方差矩阵的隐私保护估计提供了理论框架，揭示了隐私保护带来的维度相关成本，提出的隐私van Trees不等式具有更广泛的适用性，为一般隐私估计问题提供了理论工具。

Abstract: The covariance matrix plays a fundamental role in the analysis of high-dimensional data. This paper studies minimax and adaptive estimation of high-dimensional bandable covariance matrices under differential privacy constraints. We propose a novel differentially private blockwise tridiagonal estimator that achieves minimax-optimal convergence rates under both the operator norm and the Frobenius norm. In contrast to the non-private setting, the privacy-induced error exhibits a polynomial dependence on the ambient dimension, revealing a substantial additional cost of privacy.
  To establish optimality, we develop a new differentially private van Trees inequality and construct carefully designed prior distributions to obtain matching minimax lower bounds. The proposed private van Trees inequality applies more broadly to general private estimation problems and is of independent interest. We further introduce an adaptive estimator that attains the optimal rate up to a logarithmic factor without prior knowledge of the decay parameter, based on a novel hierarchical tridiagonal approach. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and illustrate the fundamental privacy-accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [124] [Uncertainty Quantification Via the Posterior Predictive Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19804)
*Sanjay Chaudhuri,Dean Dustin,Bertrand Clarke*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 使用全方差定律生成后验预测方差的多重展开式，量化预测不确定性的来源，通过分析各项贡献来评估预测模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要量化后验预测不确定性的来源，理解预测区间长度的主要贡献因素，为预测模型评估提供系统方法

Method: 利用全方差定律生成后验预测方差的多重展开式，这些展开式包含条件期望和条件方差的项，通过分析各项的绝对或相对贡献来量化不确定性来源

Result: 建立了后验预测方差的多重展开框架，能够识别不同展开式中各项之间的关联关系，当某一展开式中的特定项较小时，可以确定其他展开式中必须同样较小的项

Conclusion: 该方法为预测模型评估提供了系统化的分析工具，能够深入理解预测不确定性的来源，并在多个经典模型中得到了验证

Abstract: We use the law of total variance to generate multiple expansions for the posterior predictive variance. These expansions are sums of terms involving conditional expectations and conditional variances and provide a quantification of the sources of predictive uncertainty. Since the posterior predictive variance is fixed given the model, it represents a constant quantity that is conserved over these expansions. The terms in the expansions can be assessed in absolute or relative sense to understand the main contributors to the length of prediction intervals. We quantify the term-wise uncertainty across expansions varying in the number of terms and the order of conditionates. In particular, given that a specific term in one expansion is small or zero, we identify the other terms in other expansions that must also be small or zero. We illustrate this approach to predictive model assessment in several well-known models.

</details>


### [125] [The monotonicity of the Franz-Parisi potential is equivalent with Low-degree MMSE lower bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20070)
*Konstantinos Tsirkas,Leda Wang,Ilias Zadik*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文建立了统计物理中的Franz-Parisi势单调性与低阶多项式方法之间的数学等价关系，为理解统计估计问题的计算复杂性提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决统计物理文献（通过Franz-Parisi势的局部稳定性和单调性预测算法难度）与数学严谨文献（通过低阶多项式估计器的局限性表征难度）之间长期存在的开放问题，建立两者之间的精确数学关系。

Method: 针对高斯加性模型（GAMs）家族，证明低阶多项式的计算能力与退火Franz-Parisi势的单调性等价。在低阶猜想成立的条件下，将多项式时间极限与FP势单调性直接联系起来。

Result: 对于具有信噪比λ的高斯加性模型，低阶多项式的计算能力与退火Franz-Parisi势的单调性等价。这意味着这些模型的多项式时间极限可以直接由退火FP势的单调性推导出来。

Conclusion: 该研究首次建立了统计物理预测与低阶多项式方法之间的精确数学等价关系，验证了自1990年代以来物理学文献的预测，为理解统计估计问题的计算复杂性提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Over the last decades, two distinct approaches have been instrumental to our understanding of the computational complexity of statistical estimation. The statistical physics literature predicts algorithmic hardness through local stability and monotonicity properties of the Franz--Parisi (FP) potential \cite{franz1995recipes,franz1997phase}, while the mathematically rigorous literature characterizes hardness via the limitations of restricted algorithmic classes, most notably low-degree polynomial estimators \cite{hopkins2017efficient}. For many inference models, these two perspectives yield strikingly consistent predictions, giving rise to a long-standing open problem of establishing a precise mathematical relationship between them. In this work, we show that for estimation problems the power of low-degree polynomials is equivalent to the monotonicity of the annealed FP potential for a broad family of Gaussian additive models (GAMs) with signal-to-noise ratio $λ$. In particular, subject to a low-degree conjecture for GAMs, our results imply that the polynomial-time limits of these models are directly implied by the monotonicity of the annealed FP potential, in conceptual agreement with predictions from the physics literature dating back to the 1990s.

</details>


### [126] [Inference in high-dimensional logistic regression under tensor network dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20082)
*Josh Miles,Sohom Bhattacharya*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出高维逻辑回归中处理马尔可夫随机场依赖性的统计推断方法，超越成对交互模型，支持高阶张量结构，通过正则化伪极大似然估计和偏差校正实现渐近正态推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注高维逻辑回归的估计问题，或仅限于成对交互模型（如Ising模型），缺乏对更一般高阶依赖结构的统计推断方法，特别是在存在马尔可夫随机场诱导的个体间依赖性的情况下。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段构建正则化伪极大似然估计器，在高维特征下建立一致性；第二阶段引入偏差校正，获得渐近正态的估计器，可用于构建置信区间和假设检验。方法支持超越成对交互的高阶张量依赖结构。

Result: 理论分析表明正则化伪极大似然估计器具有一致性，偏差校正后的估计器具有渐近正态性。仿真研究证实了所提方法的有效性，能够为低维线性和二次泛函提供有效的统计推断。

Conclusion: 该方法扩展了高维逻辑回归的统计推断能力，从仅关注估计或仅限于成对交互模型，扩展到支持更一般高阶依赖结构的有效推断，为存在马尔可夫随机场依赖性的高维数据提供了实用的推断工具。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of statistical inference for logistic regression with high-dimensional covariates in settings where dependence among individuals is induced by an underlying Markov random field. Going beyond the pairwise interaction models such as the Ising model, we consider a framework to accommodate more general tensor structures that capture higher-order dependencies. We develop a two-step procedure for low-dimensional linear and quadratic functionals. The first step constructs a regularized maximum pseudolikelihood estimator, for which we establish consistency under high-dimensional features. However, as in other classical high-dimensional regression problems, this estimator is biased and cannot be directly used for valid statistical inference. The second step introduces a bias-correction that yields an asymptotically normal estimator from which one can construct confidence intervals and test hypotheses. Our results move beyond the existing literature, where only estimation guarantees were available or only for pairwise interaction models. We complement our theoretical analysis with simulation studies confirming the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [127] [Classifier-Based Nonparametric Sequential Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20135)
*Chia-Yu Hsu,Shubhanshu Shekhar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一种基于离线数据训练多分类器的序列单边检验方法，用于判断新数据流来自零假设分布还是备择假设分布，并能识别具体分布类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当零假设和备择假设分布通过历史或离线数据间接指定时的序列检验问题。传统方法通常假设分布参数已知，而实际应用中分布信息往往来自有限的历史数据。

Method: 使用离线数据集训练多分类器，基于分类器输出设计水平α的单边检验。在分布和分类器满足"可分性"条件下，构建序列检验程序，既能拒绝零假设又能识别真实分布。

Result: 获得了检验程序期望停止时间的上界，证明该上界在一般情况下不可改进。建立了确保分类器可分性的充分条件，分析了离线数据集大小和分类器族对检验性能的影响。

Conclusion: 基于分类器的序列检验方法能有效利用离线数据解决分布间接指定的检验问题，理论结果得到合成和真实数据的实证支持，并可扩展到训练-测试分布不匹配和序列变化检测等应用场景。

Abstract: We consider the problem of constructing sequential power-one tests where the null and alternative classes are specified indirectly through historical or offline data. More specifically, given an offline dataset consisting of observations from $L+1$ distributions $\{P_0, P_1, \ldots, P_L\}$, and a new unlabeled data stream $\{X_t: t \geq 1\} \overset{i.i.d}{\sim} P_θ$, the goal is to decide between the null $H_0: θ= 0$, against the alternative $H_1: θ\in [L]:=\{1,\ldots,L\}$. Our main methodological contribution is a general approach for designing a level-$α$ power-one test for this problem using a multi-class classifier trained on the given offline dataset.
  Working under a mild "separability" condition on the distributions and the trained classifier, we obtain an upper bound on the expected stopping time of our proposed level-$α$ test, and then show that in general this cannot be improved. In addition to rejecting the null, we show that our procedure can also identify the true underlying distribution almost surely. We then establish a sufficient condition to ensure the required separability of the classifier, and provide some converse results to investigate the role of the size of the offline dataset and the family of classifiers among classifier-based tests that satisfy the level-$α$ power-one criterion. Finally, we present an extension of our analysis for the training-and-testing distribution mismatch and illustrate an application to sequential change detection. Empirical results using both synthetic and real data provide support for our theoretical results.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Speculating Experts Accelerates Inference for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19289)
*Vivan Madan,Prajwal Singhania,Abhinav Bhatele,Tom Goldstein,Ashwinee Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家预取方案，利用当前计算内部表示预测未来专家，实现内存传输与计算重叠，减少MoE模型推理时的CPU-GPU传输瓶颈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在内存受限推理场景中，专家权重需卸载到CPU，导致CPU-GPU传输成为解码性能瓶颈

Method: 1. 利用当前计算的内部模型表示预测未来专家；2. 实现内存传输与计算重叠；3. 对推测执行精度不足的情况，引入轻量级估计器提高专家预测命中率

Result: 1. 证明内部表示可可靠预测未来专家；2. 推测执行保持下游任务精度；3. 在优化推理引擎中实现输出token时间减少14%；4. 轻量级估计器减少性能退化

Conclusion: 专家预取方案有效缓解MoE模型推理时的内存传输瓶颈，通过推测执行和预取实现计算-内存重叠，显著提升推理效率

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have gained popularity as a means of scaling the capacity of large language models (LLMs) while maintaining sparse activations and reduced per-token compute. However, in memory-constrained inference settings, expert weights must be offloaded to CPU, creating a performance bottleneck from CPU-GPU transfers during decoding. We propose an expert prefetching scheme that leverages currently computed internal model representations to speculate future experts, enabling memory transfers to overlap with computation. Across multiple MoE architectures, we demonstrate that future experts can be reliably predicted by these internal representations. We also demonstrate that executing speculated experts generally maintains downstream task accuracy, thus preserving more effective compute-memory overlap by eliminating the need to re-fetch true router-selected experts. Integrated into an optimized inference engine, our approach achieves up to 14\% reduction in time per output token (TPOT) over on-demand loading of experts from CPU memory. For MoEs where speculative execution alone yields suboptimal accuracy, we further examine lightweight estimators that improve expert prediction hit rates, thereby reducing performance degradation. Our code is released in open-source at https://github.com/axonn-ai/yalis/tree/offload_prefetch.

</details>


### [129] [A Visualization for Comparative Analysis of Regression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19291)
*Nassime Mountasir,Baptiste Lafabregue,Bruno Albert,Nicolas Lachiche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的回归模型性能可视化方法，通过二维残差空间、马氏距离和百分位色彩映射来更全面地评估和比较模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归性能指标（如MAE、RMSE、R²）虽然广泛使用，但过于聚合信息，无法揭示误差分布细节和模型间的复杂关系，需要更细致的可视化方法来补充

Method: 构建三维可视化方法：1）在二维空间中同时展示两个模型的残差；2）使用马氏距离考虑数据相关性和尺度差异；3）采用百分位色彩映射显示误差分布密度和异常值

Result: 该方法能够更详细地展示模型性能差异和误差分布模式，揭示传统聚合指标可能掩盖的模式，提供更全面的模型评估和比较视角

Conclusion: 提出的可视化方法增强了回归模型性能评估过程，通过图形化表示误差分布和相关关系，帮助用户获得比传统聚合指标更深入的理解

Abstract: As regression is a widely studied problem, many methods have been proposed to solve it, each of them often requiring setting different hyper-parameters. Therefore, selecting the proper method for a given application may be very difficult and relies on comparing their performances. Performance is usually measured using various metrics such as Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), or R-squared (R${}^2$). These metrics provide a numerical summary of predictive accuracy by quantifying the difference between predicted and actual values. However, while these metrics are widely used in the literature for summarizing model performance and useful to distinguish between models performing poorly and well, they often aggregate too much information. This article addresses these limitations by introducing a novel visualization approach that highlights key aspects of regression model performance. The proposed method builds upon three main contributions: (1) considering the residuals in a 2D space, which allows for simultaneous evaluation of errors from two models, (2) leveraging the Mahalanobis distance to account for correlations and differences in scale within the data, and (3) employing a colormap to visualize the percentile-based distribution of errors, making it easier to identify dense regions and outliers. By graphically representing the distribution of errors and their correlations, this approach provides a more detailed and comprehensive view of model performance, enabling users to uncover patterns that traditional aggregate metrics may obscure. The proposed visualization method facilitates a deeper understanding of regression model performance differences and error distributions, enhancing the evaluation and comparison process.

</details>


### [130] [Maximizing mutual information between user-contexts and responses improve LLM personalization with no additional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19294)
*Hyunji Nam,Haoran Li,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIPO是一种无需外部监督的自改进框架，通过对比数据增强构建偏好对，最大化提示与模型响应之间的互信息，在个性化和推理任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法严重依赖人工标注数据或外部验证器，高质量数据收集成本高昂。真正的智能远超出易验证任务范畴，因此需要无需外部监督的自改进框架。

Method: 提出互信息偏好优化(MIPO)：通过对比数据增强构建偏好对，正响应基于正确提示生成，负响应基于随机无关提示生成。使用DPO从配对数据中学习，最大化提示与模型响应之间的点条件互信息。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen-Instruct模型上的实验表明：1) 用于最大化用户上下文与响应间互信息时，MIPO成为有效个性化技术，在真实用户数据集上相比强基线提升3-40%；2) 无需额外数据或人工监督，在数学和多选题任务上提升1-18%。

Conclusion: MIPO展示了无需外部监督的自改进潜力，为LLM自我提升提供了有前景的方向，特别是在个性化和推理任务上表现出色。

Abstract: While post-training has successfully improved large language models (LLMs) across a variety of domains, these gains heavily rely on human-labeled data or external verifiers. Existing data has already been exploited, and new high-quality data is expensive to collect. More fundamentally, true intelligence goes far beyond tasks that are easily verifiable. Therefore, we need self-improvement frameworks that allow models to improve without external oversight. We propose *Mutual Information Preference Optimization (MIPO)*, a contrastive data augmentation method that constructs preference pairs by generating a positive response conditioning on the correct prompt, and a negative response by conditioning on a random, unrelated prompt. We show that using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to learn from this paired data maximizes pointwise conditional mutual information (MI) (under the base LLM) between prompts and model responses. Empirical results with various-sized Llama- and Qwen-Instruct models show that when used to maximize MI between user context and response, MIPO provides an effective personalization technique, achieving 3-40% improvements on personalization tasks using real-user datasets compared to strong baselines. Surprisingly, MIPO can also be applied to improve performance on math and multiple-choice problems, yielding 1-18% **without any additional data or human supervision**. These results suggest a promising direction for self-improvement.

</details>


### [131] [Parameter-Efficient Token Embedding Editing for Clinical Class-Level Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19302)
*Iyad Ait Hou,Shrenik Borad,Harsh Sharma,Pooja Srinivasan,Rebecca Hwa,Aya Zirikly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STEU是一种参数高效的临床语言模型遗忘方法，仅更新PMI选择的词嵌入和小分类头，实现目标类别遗忘同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型需要遵守隐私法规和机构政策，可能要求在不从头重新训练的情况下从部署系统中删除敏感信息。删除请求需要在有效遗忘目标信息与保持模型效用和最小参数修改之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了稀疏词嵌入遗忘（STEU）方法，这是一种参数高效的行为类级遗忘方法。该方法仅更新PMI选择的词嵌入以及一个小型分类头，同时保持所有编码器层冻结不变。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、MIMIC-III和eICU数据集上使用BioClinicalBERT、BERT-base和DistilBERT进行实验，STEU始终能抑制目标类别，同时很大程度上保持保留任务的性能。在主要MIMIC-IV设置中，STEU实现了近乎完全的遗忘（遗忘F1=0.0004），同时保持有竞争力的保留效用（保留平均F1=0.4766），仅修改了0.19%的模型参数。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，通过稀疏的词嵌入编辑而不修改更深层的编码器表示，可以实现有针对性的行为遗忘。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is increasingly important for clinical language models, where privacy regulations and institutional policies may require removing sensitive information from deployed systems without retraining from scratch. In practice, deletion requests must balance effective forgetting of targeted information with preservation of model utility and minimal parameter modification. We introduce Sparse Token Embedding Unlearning (STEU), a parameter-efficient method for behavioral class-level unlearning that updates only PMI-selected token embeddings together with a small classifier head while keeping all encoder layers frozen. Across experiments on MIMIC-IV, MIMIC-III, and eICU using BioClinicalBERT, BERT-base, and DistilBERT, STEU consistently suppresses the target class while largely preserving retained task performance. In the primary MIMIC-IV setting, STEU achieves near-complete forgetting (forget F1 = 0.0004) while maintaining competitive retained utility (retain avg F1 = 0.4766) after modifying only 0.19\% of model parameters. These results suggest that targeted behavioral unlearning can be achieved through sparse embedding edits without modifying deeper encoder representations.

</details>


### [132] [FalconBC: Flow matching for Amortized inference of Latent-CONditioned physiologic Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19331)
*Chloe H. Choi,Alison L. Marsden,Daniele E. Schiavazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于概率流的摊销推理框架，用于心血管建模中的边界条件调优，能同时处理临床目标、流入特征和解剖结构嵌入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动变分推理方法在两种重要场景下存在不足：已知平均流量和波形形状的开环模型，以及受血管病变影响、分割会影响压力或流量分配目标可达性的解剖结构。这两种情况下边界条件无法单独调优。

Method: 引入基于概率流的通用摊销推理框架，将临床目标、流入特征和患者特定解剖的点云嵌入作为条件变量或联合估计量，能够同时处理多种边界条件调优场景。

Result: 在两个患者特定模型上验证了方法：具有不同狭窄位置和严重程度的主动脉-髂动脉分叉，以及冠状动脉树。

Conclusion: 提出的概率流框架能够有效解决传统方法在复杂心血管建模场景中的局限性，为边界条件调优提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Boundary condition tuning is a fundamental step in patient-specific cardiovascular modeling. Despite an increase in offline training cost, recent methods in data-driven variational inference can efficiently estimate the joint posterior distribution of boundary conditions, with amortization of training efforts over clinical targets. However, even the most modern approaches fall short in two important scenarios: open-loop models with known mean flow and assumed waveform shapes, and anatomies affected by vascular lesions where segmentation influences the reachability of pressure or flow split targets. In both cases, boundary conditions cannot be tuned in isolation. We introduce a general amortized inference framework based on probabilistic flow that treats clinical targets, inflow features, and point cloud embeddings of patient-specific anatomies as either conditioning variables or quantities to be jointly estimated. We demonstrate the approach on two patient-specific models: an aorto-iliac bifurcation with varying stenosis locations and severity, and a coronary arterial tree.

</details>


### [133] [Alternating Diffusion for Proximal Sampling with Zeroth Order Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19633)
*Hirohane Takagi,Atsushi Nitanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用势函数零阶信息的近似近端采样器，通过直接模拟动力学而非拒绝采样实现后向步骤，将中间粒子分布建模为高斯混合，从而从可直接采样的分布中获得蒙特卡洛分数估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近端采样方法的后向步骤通常通过拒绝采样实现，而基于扩散的采样方法需要通过学习模型或调用辅助采样器来估计分数。本文旨在开发一种仅使用势函数零阶信息、避免拒绝采样、具有确定性运行时间预算的采样方法。

Method: 将中间粒子分布建模为高斯混合，直接从可采样分布中获得蒙特卡洛分数估计器。通过直接模拟动力学而非拒绝采样实现近端采样的后向步骤，算法避免拒绝采样，允许灵活的步长设置，并在确定性运行时间预算下运行。

Result: 理论上，当分数估计误差得到充分控制时，该方法继承了在目标分布等周条件下近端采样的指数收敛性。数值实验表明，该方法通过多个粒子间的相互作用和并行计算利用，能够快速收敛到目标分布。

Conclusion: 提出了一种仅需势函数零阶信息的近似近端采样器，通过直接模拟动力学和将中间分布建模为高斯混合，避免了拒绝采样，具有灵活步长和确定性运行时间，在理论和实践中均表现出色。

Abstract: This work introduces a new approximate proximal sampler that operates solely with zeroth-order information of the potential function. Prior theoretical analyses have revealed that proximal sampling corresponds to alternating forward and backward iterations of the heat flow. The backward step was originally implemented by rejection sampling, whereas we directly simulate the dynamics. Unlike diffusion-based sampling methods that estimate scores via learned models or by invoking auxiliary samplers, our method treats the intermediate particle distribution as a Gaussian mixture, thereby yielding a Monte Carlo score estimator from directly samplable distributions. Theoretically, when the score estimation error is sufficiently controlled, our method inherits the exponential convergence of proximal sampling under isoperimetric conditions on the target distribution. In practice, the algorithm avoids rejection sampling, permits flexible step sizes, and runs with a deterministic runtime budget. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our approach converges rapidly to the target distribution, driven by interactions among multiple particles and by exploiting parallel computation.

</details>


### [134] [Heavy-Tailed and Long-Range Dependent Noise in Stochastic Approximation: A Finite-Time Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19648)
*Siddharth Chandak,Anuj Yadav,Ayfer Ozgur,Nicholas Bambos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在重尾和长程依赖噪声下的随机逼近算法，首次建立了这两种非经典噪声模型下的有限时间矩界，并应用于SGD和梯度博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机逼近分析通常基于鞅差或马尔可夫噪声，但金融、通信等实际应用中经常遇到重尾和长程依赖噪声，现有理论无法处理这些非经典噪声模型。

Method: 采用噪声平均化论证方法，在不修改迭代过程的情况下正则化噪声影响，研究强单调算子求根问题，建立有限时间矩界。

Result: 首次建立了重尾和长程依赖噪声下的有限时间矩界，提供了显式收敛速率，量化了重尾和时序依赖的影响。

Conclusion: 提出的通用框架可处理非经典噪声模型，成功应用于SGD和梯度博弈，数值实验验证了有限时间分析的有效性。

Abstract: Stochastic approximation (SA) is a fundamental iterative framework with broad applications in reinforcement learning and optimization. Classical analyses typically rely on martingale difference or Markov noise with bounded second moments, but many practical settings, including finance and communications, frequently encounter heavy-tailed and long-range dependent (LRD) noise. In this work, we study SA for finding the root of a strongly monotone operator under these non-classical noise models. We establish the first finite-time moment bounds in both settings, providing explicit convergence rates that quantify the impact of heavy tails and temporal dependence. Our analysis employs a noise-averaging argument that regularizes the impact of noise without modifying the iteration. Finally, we apply our general framework to stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and gradient play, and corroborate our finite-time analysis through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [135] [Scalable Learning of Multivariate Distributions via Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19792)
*Zeyu Ding,Katja Ickstadt,Nadja Klein,Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多元条件转换模型（MCTMs）的新型核心集构建方法，通过重要性采样实现数据大幅缩减，在保持统计精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非参数和半参数回归分析与密度估计在统计学和机器学习中至关重要，但现有方法难以处理大规模数据。需要开发能够增强多元条件转换模型可扩展性和训练效率的方法。

Method: 开发了针对半参数分布模型的首个核心集构建方法，基于重要性采样实现数据缩减。采用基于输入数据凸包的几何近似来解决对数项归一化的数值问题，确保在大数据场景下的可行、稳定和准确推断。

Result: 该方法能以高概率保证对数似然保持在(1±ε)的乘法误差范围内，从而保持统计模型精度。数值实验表明在处理大规模复杂数据集时计算效率显著提升。

Conclusion: 该核心集方法为半参数分布模型提供了首个可扩展解决方案，增强了模型在复杂分布和非线性关系场景中的适应性，为统计学和机器学习社区的广泛应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Efficient and scalable non-parametric or semi-parametric regression analysis and density estimation are of crucial importance to the fields of statistics and machine learning. However, available methods are limited in their ability to handle large-scale data. We address this issue by developing a novel coreset construction for multivariate conditional transformation models (MCTMs) to enhance their scalability and training efficiency. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first coresets for semi-parametric distributional models. Our approach yields substantial data reduction via importance sampling. It ensures with high probability that the log-likelihood remains within multiplicative error bounds of $(1\pm\varepsilon)$ and thereby maintains statistical model accuracy. Compared to conventional full-parametric models, where coresets have been incorporated before, our semi-parametric approach exhibits enhanced adaptability, particularly in scenarios where complex distributions and non-linear relationships are present, but not fully understood. To address numerical problems associated with normalizing logarithmic terms, we follow a geometric approximation based on the convex hull of input data. This ensures feasible, stable, and accurate inference in scenarios involving large amounts of data. Numerical experiments demonstrate substantially improved computational efficiency when handling large and complex datasets, thus laying the foundation for a broad range of applications within the statistics and machine learning communities.

</details>


### [136] [LeWorldModel: Stable End-to-End Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture from Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19312)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeWorldModel (LeWM) 是一种稳定的端到端联合嵌入预测架构，仅使用两个损失项从原始像素训练世界模型，无需复杂正则化或预训练编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法存在脆弱性，依赖复杂的多损失项、指数移动平均、预训练编码器或辅助监督来避免表示崩溃，需要更简单稳定的训练方法。

Method: 提出LeWM，仅使用两个损失项：下一嵌入预测损失和强制潜在嵌入符合高斯分布的正则化器。将可调损失超参数从6个减少到1个，实现端到端稳定训练。

Result: LeWM（约1500万参数）在单GPU上几小时可训练，规划速度比基于基础模型的世界模型快48倍，在多种2D和3D控制任务中保持竞争力。潜在空间编码了有意义的物理结构，并能可靠检测物理上不可信的事件。

Conclusion: LeWM证明了JEPA框架可以通过极简的损失设计实现稳定训练，为紧凑潜在空间中的世界模型学习提供了更简单有效的解决方案，在控制任务和物理理解方面都表现出色。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) offer a compelling framework for learning world models in compact latent spaces, yet existing methods remain fragile, relying on complex multi-term losses, exponential moving averages, pre-trained encoders, or auxiliary supervision to avoid representation collapse. In this work, we introduce LeWorldModel (LeWM), the first JEPA that trains stably end-to-end from raw pixels using only two loss terms: a next-embedding prediction loss and a regularizer enforcing Gaussian-distributed latent embeddings. This reduces tunable loss hyperparameters from six to one compared to the only existing end-to-end alternative. With ~15M parameters trainable on a single GPU in a few hours, LeWM plans up to 48x faster than foundation-model-based world models while remaining competitive across diverse 2D and 3D control tasks. Beyond control, we show that LeWM's latent space encodes meaningful physical structure through probing of physical quantities. Surprise evaluation confirms that the model reliably detects physically implausible events.

</details>


### [137] [A General Deep Learning Framework for Wireless Resource Allocation under Discrete Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19322)
*Yikun Wang,Yang Li,Yik-Chung Wu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于支持集的深度学习框架，通过概率建模解决无线资源分配中离散变量的零梯度、约束满足和非SPSD属性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在连续无线资源分配中表现出色，但在处理离散变量时面临三大挑战：反向传播的零梯度问题、复杂离散约束的难以实施、以及无法生成具有非相同参数相同决策（non-SPSD）属性的解

Method: 引入支持集表示离散变量，将其元素建模为随机变量并学习其联合概率分布；通过将联合概率分解为条件概率的乘积，顺序学习每个条件概率；使用动态上下文嵌入捕获演化的离散解

Result: 在两种代表性混合离散无线资源分配问题（无小区系统中的联合用户关联与波束成形、可移动天线系统中的联合天线定位与波束成形）上，所提框架在系统性能和计算效率方面均优于现有基线方法

Conclusion: 提出的基于概率建模的深度学习框架有效解决了离散变量处理的三大核心挑战，为混合离散无线资源分配问题提供了通用且高效的解决方案

Abstract: While deep learning (DL)-based methods have achieved remarkable success in continuous wireless resource allocation, efficient solutions for problems involving discrete variables remain challenging. This is primarily due to the zero-gradient issue in backpropagation, the difficulty of enforcing intricate constraints with discrete variables, and the inability in generating solutions with non-same-parameter-same-decision (non-SPSD) property. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a general DL framework by introducing the support set to represent the discrete variables. We model the elements of the support set as random variables and learn their joint probability distribution. By factorizing the joint probability as the product of conditional probabilities, each conditional probability is sequentially learned. This probabilistic modeling directly tackles all the aforementioned challenges of DL for handling discrete variables. By operating on probability distributions instead of hard binary decisions, the framework naturally avoids the zero-gradient issue. During the learning of the conditional probabilities, discrete constraints can be seamlessly enforced by masking out infeasible solutions. Moreover, with a dynamic context embedding that captures the evolving discrete solutions, the non-SPSD property is inherently provided by the proposed framework. We apply the proposed framework to two representative mixed-discrete wireless resource allocation problems: (a) joint user association and beamforming in cell-free systems, and (b) joint antenna positioning and beamforming in movable antenna-aided systems. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed DL framework consistently outperforms existing baselines in terms of both system performance and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [138] [Target Concept Tuning Improves Extreme Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19325)
*Shijie Ren,Xinyue Gu,Ziheng Peng,Haifan Zhang,Peisong Niu,Bo Wu,Xiting Wang,Liang Sun,Jirong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TaCT框架，通过概念门控微调解决气象预报中罕见但高影响事件（如台风）的预测问题，在保持整体性能的同时针对失败案例进行选择性模型改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习气象预报模型在罕见但高影响事件（如台风）上表现不佳，因为相关数据稀缺。现有微调方法面临两难：要么忽略这些极端事件，要么过度拟合它们而损害整体性能。

Method: 提出TaCT框架：1) 使用稀疏自编码器和反事实分析自动发现与失败案例相关的内部概念；2) 仅当相应概念被激活时才更新参数，而非统一适应；3) 实现选择性模型改进。

Result: 实验显示在不同区域的台风预报中均取得一致改进，且不降低其他气象变量的预测性能。识别的概念对应有物理意义的环流模式，揭示了模型偏差并支持科学预报任务中的可信适应。

Conclusion: TaCT框架通过概念门控微调解决了罕见高影响事件预报的难题，实现了在保持整体性能的同时针对失败案例的选择性改进，为科学预报任务提供了可解释且可信的适应方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models for meteorological forecasting often fail in rare but high-impact events such as typhoons, where relevant data is scarce. Existing fine-tuning methods typically face a trade-off between overlooking these extreme events and overfitting them at the expense of overall performance. We propose TaCT, an interpretable concept-gated fine-tuning framework that solves the aforementioned issue by selective model improvement: models are adapted specifically for failure cases while preserving performance in common scenarios. To this end, TaCT automatically discovers failure-related internal concepts using Sparse Autoencoders and counterfactual analysis, and updates parameters only when the corresponding concepts are activated, rather than applying uniform adaptation. Experiments show consistent improvements in typhoon forecasting across different regions without degrading other meteorological variables. The identified concepts correspond to physically meaningful circulation patterns, revealing model biases and supporting trustworthy adaptation in scientific forecasting tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Concept-Gated-Fine-tune-62AC.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Single Tokens: Distilling Discrete Diffusion Models via Discrete MMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20155)
*Emiel Hoogeboom,David Ruhe,Jonathan Heek,Thomas Mensink,Tim Salimans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出离散矩匹配蒸馏方法，解决离散扩散模型蒸馏困难问题，在文本和图像数据集上实现高质量多样化生成，蒸馏后的生成器性能可超越原模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型蒸馏困难，而连续扩散模型已有多种蒸馏方法可将采样步骤减少到少数几步。需要将连续域的成功思想应用于离散扩散模型蒸馏

Method: 离散矩匹配蒸馏方法，借鉴连续域的成功思想，通过矩匹配技术进行蒸馏，避免先前离散蒸馏方法的性能崩溃问题

Result: 在文本和图像数据集上，D-MMD方法在足够采样步骤下保持高质量和多样性，新蒸馏的生成器性能可超越原始教师模型

Conclusion: 离散矩匹配蒸馏方法成功解决了离散扩散模型蒸馏难题，将连续域蒸馏技术有效迁移到离散域，为离散扩散模型的高效采样提供了可行方案

Abstract: It is currently difficult to distill discrete diffusion models. In contrast, continuous diffusion literature has many distillation approaches methods that can reduce sampling steps to a handful.
  Our method, Discrete Moment Matching Distillation (D-MMD), leverages ideas that have been highly successful in the continuous domain. Whereas previous discrete distillation methods collapse, D-MMD maintains high quality and diversity (given sufficient sampling steps). This is demonstrated on both text and image datasets. Moreover, the newly distilled generators can outperform their teachers.

</details>


### [140] [BrainSCL: Subtype-Guided Contrastive Learning for Brain Disorder Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19295)
*Xiaolong Li,Guiliang Guo,Guangqi Wen,Peng Cao,Jinzhu Yang,Honglin Wu,Xiaoli Liu,Fei Wang,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种亚型引导的对比学习框架，通过建模患者异质性作为潜在亚型，结合多模态表征和原型注意力机制，提升精神障碍分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神障碍人群存在显著的异质性，这种样本间的显著差异给对比学习中正样本对的构建带来挑战。传统方法难以有效处理这种异质性，需要新的框架来建模患者亚型并利用这些结构先验指导表示学习。

Method: 提出亚型引导对比学习框架：1) 结合临床文本和从BOLD信号自适应学习的图结构构建多视图表征；2) 通过无监督谱聚类发现潜在亚型；3) 设计双级注意力机制构建原型，捕获稳定的亚型特异性连接模式；4) 提出亚型引导对比学习策略，将样本拉向其亚型原型图，增强亚型内一致性。

Result: 在重度抑郁症(MDD)、双相情感障碍(BD)和自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)上进行评估。实验结果表明亚型原型图能有效指导对比学习，所提方法在性能上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的亚型引导对比学习框架通过建模患者异质性作为潜在亚型，并利用这些结构先验指导表示学习，有效解决了精神障碍人群异质性带来的挑战，提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Mental disorder populations exhibit pronounced heterogeneity -- that is, the significant differences between samples -- poses a significant challenge to the definition of positive pairs in contrastive learning. To address this, we propose a subtype-guided contrastive learning framework that models patient heterogeneity as latent subtypes and incorporates them as structural priors to guide discriminative representation learning. Specifically, we construct multi-view representations by combining patients' clinical text with graph structure adaptively learned from BOLD signals, to uncover latent subtypes via unsupervised spectral clustering. A dual-level attention mechanism is proposed to construct prototypes for capturing stable subtype-specific connectivity patterns. We further propose a subtype-guided contrastive learning strategy that pulls samples toward their subtype prototype graph, reinforcing intra-subtype consistency for providing effective supervisory signals to improve model performance. We evaluate our method on Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), Bipolar Disorder (BD), and Autism Spectrum Disorders (ASD). Experimental results confirm the effectiveness of subtype prototype graphs in guiding contrastive learning and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art approaches. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BrainSCL-06D7.

</details>


### [141] [Global Convergence of Multiplicative Updates for the Matrix Mechanism: A Collaborative Proof with Gemini 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19465)
*Keith Rush*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了一个涉及Hadamard积结构的正则化核范数目标优化中固定点迭代的收敛性，填补了文献中的空白，并展示了AI在数学证明中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于解决Denisov等人提出的私有机器学习算法空间优化问题中遗留的数学问题。具体来说，需要证明一个涉及Hadamard积结构的正则化核范数目标优化中固定点迭代的收敛性，这个问题在先前工作中被提出但未得到证明。

Method: 采用固定点迭代方法，分析迭代过程$v^{(k+1)} = \text{diag}((D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2} M D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2})^{1/2})$的收敛性。证明的核心部分由Gemini 3 AI系统提供，经过人工修正和干预。论文还详细描述了AI辅助证明的提示过程和协作原则。

Result: 证明了迭代过程单调收敛到势函数$J(v) = 2 \text{Tr}((D_v^{1/2} M D_v^{1/2})^{1/2}) - \sum v_i$的唯一全局最优解，从而填补了文献中的空白。同时展示了AI在数学证明中的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅解决了私有机器学习优化问题中的一个数学理论问题，更重要的是展示了AI在数学研究中的实际应用潜力。论文提供了AI辅助数学证明的方法论和协作原则，为数学研究的新范式提供了案例。

Abstract: We analyze a fixed-point iteration $v \leftarrow φ(v)$ arising in the optimization of a regularized nuclear norm objective involving the Hadamard product structure, posed in~\cite{denisov} in the context of an optimization problem over the space of algorithms in private machine learning. We prove that the iteration $v^{(k+1)} = \text{diag}((D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2} M D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2})^{1/2})$ converges monotonically to the unique global optimizer of the potential function $J(v) = 2 \text{Tr}((D_v^{1/2} M D_v^{1/2})^{1/2}) - \sum v_i$, closing a problem left open there.
  The bulk of this proof was provided by Gemini 3, subject to some corrections and interventions. Gemini 3 also sketched the initial version of this note. Thus, it represents as much a commentary on the practical use of AI in mathematics as it represents the closure of a small gap in the literature. As such, we include a small narrative description of the prompting process, and some resulting principles for working with AI to prove mathematics.

</details>


### [142] [TTQ: Activation-Aware Test-Time Quantization to Accelerate LLM Inference On The Fly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19296)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出测试时量化框架，在推理时动态压缩大模型，解决传统方法依赖校准数据导致的领域偏移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础模型计算需求巨大，现有激活感知压缩技术依赖校准数据，在未见下游任务上存在领域偏移问题

Method: 提出测试时量化框架，通过高效在线校准实现即时激活感知量化，可适应每个提示而不依赖特定下游任务

Result: 实验表明TTQ在量化性能上优于最先进的基线方法，同时实现推理加速

Conclusion: 测试时量化框架能有效解决领域偏移问题，为大型模型提供灵活高效的压缩方案

Abstract: To tackle the huge computational demand of large foundation models, activation-aware compression techniques without retraining have been introduced. However, since these methods highly rely on calibration data, domain shift issues may arise for unseen downstream tasks. We propose a test-time quantization (TTQ) framework which compresses large models on the fly at inference time to resolve this issue. With an efficient online calibration, instant activation-aware quantization can adapt every prompt regardless of the downstream tasks, yet achieving inference speedup. Several experiments demonstrate that TTQ can improve the quantization performance over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [143] [CLaRE-ty Amid Chaos: Quantifying Representational Entanglement to Predict Ripple Effects in LLM Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19297)
*Manit Baser,Alperen Yildiz,Dinil Mon Divakaran,Mohan Gurusamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLaRE是一种轻量级的表示层技术，用于识别语言模型编辑中的涟漪效应，通过前向激活量化事实间的纠缠关系，比梯度方法更快更省内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的静态知识表示会随时间过时或错误，模型编辑技术虽然能修改事实关联，但会产生不可预测的涟漪效应（意外行为变化传播到隐藏空间）。

Method: CLaRE使用单一中间层的前向激活来量化事实间的纠缠关系，避免昂贵的反向传播。构建了包含11,427个事实的语料库，计算大规模纠缠图来捕捉局部编辑在表示空间的传播。

Result: 与基线相比，CLaRE在Spearman相关性上平均提升62.2%，速度提高2.74倍，峰值GPU内存减少2.85倍，存储需求也大幅降低。纠缠图可用于模型编辑保护集、审计追踪、高效红队测试和可扩展的后编辑评估。

Conclusion: CLaRE提供了一种高效识别模型编辑涟漪效应的方法，通过前向激活分析事实纠缠，为模型编辑提供了更强的保护机制和评估工具，相关数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: The static knowledge representations of large language models (LLMs) inevitably become outdated or incorrect over time. While model-editing techniques offer a promising solution by modifying a model's factual associations, they often produce unpredictable ripple effects, which are unintended behavioral changes that propagate even to the hidden space. In this work, we introduce CLaRE, a lightweight representation-level technique to identify where these ripple effects may occur. Unlike prior gradient-based methods, CLaRE quantifies entanglement between facts using forward activations from a single intermediate layer, avoiding costly backward passes. To enable systematic study, we prepare and analyse a corpus of 11,427 facts drawn from three existing datasets. Using CLaRE, we compute large-scale entanglement graphs of this corpus for multiple models, capturing how local edits propagate through representational space. These graphs enable stronger preservation sets for model editing, audit trails, efficient red-teaming, and scalable post-edit evaluation. In comparison to baselines, CLaRE achieves an average of 62.2% improvement in Spearman correlation with ripple effects while being $2.74\times$ faster, and using $2.85\times$ less peak GPU memory. Besides, CLaRE requires only a fraction of the storage needed by the baselines to compute and preserve fact representations. Our entanglement graphs and corpus are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CLaRE-488E.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Layerwise Perturbation: Unifying Off-Policy Corrections for LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19470)
*Chenlu Ye,Xuanchang Zhang,Yifan Hao,Zhou Yu,Ziji Zhang,Abhinav Gullapalli,Hao Chen,Jing Huang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ALP方法，通过向Transformer各层隐藏状态注入可学习扰动，解决强化学习中策略陈旧和训练-推理不匹配问题，稳定重要性比率分布，提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略强化学习中存在策略陈旧和训练-推理不匹配问题，导致重要性比率分布重尾化。当策略局部尖锐时，重要性比率尾部膨胀，产生尖锐梯度，可能使更新超出信任区域，影响训练稳定性。

Method: 提出自适应分层扰动(ALP)方法：在更新过程中向Transformer各层的输入隐藏状态注入小的可学习扰动。这些扰动作为重要性比率的分子，与未改变的推理策略形成对比。通过控制中间表示的噪声，防止更新策略与推理策略偏离过远，扩大策略族以覆盖带有不匹配噪声的推理策略族。

Result: 在单轮数学推理和多轮工具集成推理任务上的实验表明，ALP不仅提升最终性能，还能避免重要性比率尾部爆炸和KL散度尖峰，同时增强探索能力。消融实验显示，所有层的表示级扰动效果最佳，显著优于部分层扰动和仅logits扰动的变体。

Conclusion: ALP通过向隐藏状态注入可学习扰动，有效平坦化重要性比率分布，缩小更新策略与推理策略之间的差距，减少比率尾部，从而维持训练稳定性，同时提升模型性能和探索能力。

Abstract: Off-policy problems such as policy staleness and training-inference mismatch, has become a major bottleneck for training stability and further exploration for LLM RL. To enhance inference efficiency, the distribution gap between the inference and updated policy grows, leading to heavy-tailed importance ratios. Heavy-tailed ratios arise when the policy is locally sharp, which further inflates sharp gradients and can push updates outside the trust region. To address this, we propose Adaptive Layerwise Perturbation(ALP) by injecting small learnable perturbations into input hidden states of each layer during updates, which is used as the numerator of the importance ratio against the unchanged inference policy in the objective. Intuitively, by adding controlled noise to intermediate representations, ALP prevents the updated policy from deviating too sharply from the inference policy, and enlarges the policy family to cover the inference policy family with mismatch noises. Hence, the flattened distribution can naturally tighten the updated and inference policy gap and reduce the tail of importance ratios, thus maintaining training stability. This is further validated empirically. Experiments on single-turn math and multi-turn tool-integrated reasoning tasks show that ALP not only improves final performance, but also avoid blow up of importance ratio tail and KL spikes during iterative training, along with boosted exploration. Ablations show that representation-level perturbations across all layers are most effective, substantially outperforming partial-layer and logits-only variants.

</details>


### [145] [DPxFin: Adaptive Differential Privacy for Anti-Money Laundering Detection via Reputation-Weighted Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19314)
*Renuga Kanagavelu,Manjil Nepal,Ning Peiyan,Cai Kangning,Xu Jiming,Fei Gao,Yong Liu,Goh Siow Mong Rick,Qingsong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPxFin：一种用于反洗钱检测的联邦学习框架，通过信誉引导的自适应差分隐私在保护隐私的同时保持模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代金融系统中反洗钱面临数据隐私和复杂欺诈模式的挑战。联邦学习虽然能保护数据隐私，但在表格数据（如金融数据）中容易发生隐私泄露。需要一种既能保护隐私又能保持模型效用的解决方案。

Method: 提出DPxFin框架，集成信誉引导的自适应差分隐私。通过评估本地模型与全局模型的对齐度计算客户端信誉，根据信誉动态分配差分隐私噪声：高信誉客户端获得较低噪声以放大其可信贡献，低信誉客户端获得较强噪声以降低风险。

Result: 在反洗钱数据集上（IID和非IID设置）使用MLP进行验证。实验表明，相比传统联邦学习和固定噪声差分隐私基线，DPxFin在准确性和隐私性之间取得了更好的权衡，性能改进虽然幅度不大但一致。框架能抵御表格数据泄露攻击，证明其在真实金融条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: DPxFin通过信誉引导的自适应差分隐私机制，在联邦学习环境中有效解决了反洗钱检测中的隐私保护问题，实现了隐私保护与模型性能的良好平衡，适用于真实金融场景。

Abstract: In the modern financial system, combating money laundering is a critical challenge complicated by data privacy concerns and increasingly complex fraud transaction patterns. Although federated learning (FL) is a promising problem-solving approach as it allows institutions to train their models without sharing their data, it has the drawback of being prone to privacy leakage, specifically in tabular data forms like financial data. To address this, we propose DPxFin, a novel federated framework that integrates reputation-guided adaptive differential privacy. Our approach computes client reputation by evaluating the alignment between locally trained models and the global model. Based on this reputation, we dynamically assign differential privacy noise to client updates, enhancing privacy while maintaining overall model utility. Clients with higher reputations receive lower noise to amplify their trustworthy contributions, while low-reputation clients are allocated stronger noise to mitigate risk. We validate DPxFin on the Anti-Money Laundering (AML) dataset under both IID and non-IID settings using Multi Layer Perceptron (MLP). Experimental analysis established that our approach has a more desirable trade-off between accuracy and privacy than those of traditional FL and fixed-noise Differential Privacy (DP) baselines, where performance improvements were consistent, even though on a modest scale. Moreover, DPxFin does withstand tabular data leakage attacks, proving its effectiveness under real-world financial conditions.

</details>


### [146] [PRIME-CVD: A Parametrically Rendered Informatics Medical Environment for Education in Cardiovascular Risk Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19299)
*Nicholas I-Hsien Kuo,Marzia Hoque Tania,Blanca Gallego,Louisa Jorm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIME-CVD是一个参数化生成的合成心血管疾病数据集，包含5万成人队列，用于医学教育和方法学研究，避免真实患者数据的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实患者电子病历数据因隐私、治理和再识别风险难以用于教学和方法开发，限制了心血管风险建模的可重复性、透明度和实践培训。

Method: 基于用户指定的因果有向无环图，使用澳大利亚人口统计数据和流行病学效应估计参数化生成合成数据，而非基于真实患者数据或生成模型训练。

Result: 创建了两个开放访问的合成数据集：数据资产1为干净的分析就绪队列，数据资产2为具有真实结构异质性的关系型EMR风格数据库。

Conclusion: PRIME-CVD支持数据清洗、因果推理和政策相关风险建模的教学，同时保持真实的亚组不平衡和风险梯度，确保可忽略的披露风险。

Abstract: In recent years, progress in medical informatics and machine learning has been accelerated by the availability of openly accessible benchmark datasets. However, patient-level electronic medical record (EMR) data are rarely available for teaching or methodological development due to privacy, governance, and re-identification risks. This has limited reproducibility, transparency, and hands-on training in cardiovascular risk modelling. Here we introduce PRIME-CVD, a parametrically rendered informatics medical environment designed explicitly for medical education. PRIME-CVD comprises two openly accessible synthetic data assets representing a cohort of 50,000 adults undergoing primary prevention for cardiovascular disease. The datasets are generated entirely from a user-specified causal directed acyclic graph parameterised using publicly available Australian population statistics and published epidemiologic effect estimates, rather than from patient-level EMR data or trained generative models. Data Asset 1 provides a clean, analysis-ready cohort suitable for exploratory analysis, stratification, and survival modelling, while Data Asset 2 restructures the same cohort into a relational, EMR-style database with realistic structural and lexical heterogeneity. Together, these assets enable instruction in data cleaning, harmonisation, causal reasoning, and policy-relevant risk modelling without exposing sensitive information. Because all individuals and events are generated de novo, PRIME-CVD preserves realistic subgroup imbalance and risk gradients while ensuring negligible disclosure risk. PRIME-CVD is released under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 licence to support reproducible research and scalable medical education.

</details>


### [147] [Subspace Kernel Learning on Tensor Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19546)
*Lei Wang,Xi Ding,Yongsheng Gao,Piotr Koniusz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UKTL提出了一种用于多模态张量数据的核学习框架，通过不确定性驱动的子空间加权和可扩展的Nyström线性化，实现了高效、鲁棒且可解释的张量相似性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理结构化多路数据（高阶张量）需要捕捉张量模式间的复杂交互，同时保持计算效率。现有方法在表达性、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面存在局限。

Method: 提出不确定性驱动的核张量学习(UKTL)：1) 通过张量展开提取模式子空间并进行比较；2) 使用软k-means聚类获取动态枢轴张量，实现可扩展的Nyström核线性化；3) 引入不确定性感知子空间加权，自适应降低不可靠模式分量的权重。

Result: 在动作识别基准（NTU-60、NTU-120、Kinetics-Skeleton）上取得最先进性能，展示了优越的泛化能力和有意义的模式级洞察。

Conclusion: UKTL为结构化多路和多模态张量序列建立了一个原则性、可扩展且可解释的核学习范式，平衡了表达性、鲁棒性和计算效率。

Abstract: Learning from structured multi-way data, represented as higher-order tensors, requires capturing complex interactions across tensor modes while remaining computationally efficient. We introduce Uncertainty-driven Kernel Tensor Learning (UKTL), a novel kernel framework for $M$-mode tensors that compares mode-wise subspaces derived from tensor unfoldings, enabling expressive and robust similarity measure. To handle large-scale tensor data, we propose a scalable Nyström kernel linearization with dynamically learned pivot tensors obtained via soft $k$-means clustering. A key innovation of UKTL is its uncertainty-aware subspace weighting, which adaptively down-weights unreliable mode components based on estimated confidence, improving robustness and interpretability in comparisons between input and pivot tensors. Our framework is fully end-to-end trainable and naturally incorporates both multi-way and multi-mode interactions through structured kernel compositions. Extensive evaluations on action recognition benchmarks (NTU-60, NTU-120, Kinetics-Skeleton) show that UKTL achieves state-of-the-art performance, superior generalization, and meaningful mode-wise insights. This work establishes a principled, scalable, and interpretable kernel learning paradigm for structured multi-way and multi-modal tensor sequences.

</details>


### [148] [DeepStock: Reinforcement Learning with Policy Regularizations for Inventory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19621)
*Yaqi Xie,Xinru Hao,Jiaxi Liu,Will Ma,Linwei Xin,Lei Cao,Yidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过基于经典库存概念（如"基础库存"）的策略正则化，显著加速深度强化学习在库存管理中的超参数调优并提升最终性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习（DRL）为库存策略训练提供了通用方法，但现成实现效果参差不齐，对训练超参数高度敏感，需要改进

Method: 提出基于经典库存概念（如"基础库存"）的策略正则化方法，应用于多种DRL方法，并在阿里巴巴天猫平台进行100%部署验证

Result: 策略正则化显著加速超参数调优并提升最终性能，改变了关于库存管理最佳DRL方法的认知，在天猫平台成功部署

Conclusion: 基于经典库存概念的策略正则化是提升DRL在库存管理中应用效果的有效方法，能够解决超参数敏感性问题并改善性能

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) provides a general-purpose methodology for training inventory policies that can leverage big data and compute. However, off-the-shelf implementations of DRL have seen mixed success, often plagued by high sensitivity to the hyperparameters used during training. In this paper, we show that by imposing policy regularizations, grounded in classical inventory concepts such as "Base Stock", we can significantly accelerate hyperparameter tuning and improve the final performance of several DRL methods. We report details from a 100% deployment of DRL with policy regularizations on Alibaba's e-commerce platform, Tmall. We also include extensive synthetic experiments, which show that policy regularizations reshape the narrative on what is the best DRL method for inventory management.

</details>


### [149] [The Residual Stream Is All You Need: On the Redundancy of the KV Cache in Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19664)
*Kaleem Ullah Qasim,Jiashu Zhang,Muhammad Kafeel Shaheen,Razan Alharith,Heying Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV缓存并非必需状态，论文证明残差流是唯一信息载体，可通过单残差向量逐层重计算键值对，实现零误差重构，提出KV-Direct方案大幅降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer推理中广泛将KV缓存视为必需状态，大量工作致力于压缩、驱逐或近似KV缓存条目，但作者认为这种状态可能是冗余的，需要验证残差流是否足以重构所有信息。

Method: 通过理论证明每层的键值都是残差流的确定性投影，从单残差向量重计算可实现比特级零误差重构。实验验证包括：1) 在6个模型（135M-4B参数）上验证重构准确性；2) 跨任务残差修补实验验证残差流的马尔可夫性质；3) 提出KV-Direct方案，仅检查点残差向量（每token 5KB）而非完整KV对（136KB），按需重计算键值。

Result: 1) 贪婪解码下移除缓存并重计算产生完全相同的token输出；2) KV-Direct在20轮对话中将峰值内存保持在42MB，而标准缓存超过103MB；3) 相比5种驱逐基线（H2O、StreamingLLM等），KV-Direct在所有缓存预算下保持100%token匹配，基线仅5-28%；4) 中等批次大小下重计算比读取缓存张量快5倍。

Conclusion: KV缓存是冗余状态，残差流是Transformer推理中唯一必需的信息载体。KV-Direct方案通过检查点残差向量并按需重计算键值，实现了内存效率的显著提升，同时保持输出准确性，为高效Transformer推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is widely treated as essential state in transformer inference, and a large body of work engineers policies to compress, evict, or approximate its entries. We prove that this state is entirely redundant: keys and values at every layer are deterministic projections of the residual stream, and recomputing them from a single residual vector per token incurs exactly zero reconstruction error, not approximately, but bit-identically. We verify this across six models from four architecture families (135M to 4B parameters). Cross-task residual patching at every layer produces D_KL = 0 between patched and original output distributions, confirming that the residual stream satisfies a Markov property and is the sole information-carrying state. Removing the cache entirely and recomputing from scratch yields token-identical output under greedy decoding on all models tested. We build on this result with KV-Direct, a bounded-memory inference scheme that checkpoints residual vectors (5 KB per token on Gemma 3-4B) instead of full KV pairs (136 KB), recomputing keys and values on demand. Over 20 conversation turns, KV-Direct holds peak memory at 42 MB while the standard cache grows past 103 MB. Against five eviction baselines (H2O, StreamingLLM, SnapKV, TOVA, window-only), KV-Direct maintains 100% token match at every cache budget; all baselines degrade to 5-28%. A per-operation latency analysis shows recomputation runs up to 5x faster than reading cached tensors at moderate batch sizes. Code is available at https://github.com/Kaleemullahqasim/KV-Direct.

</details>


### [150] [DAPA: Distribution Aware Piecewise Activation Functions for On-Device Transformer Inference and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19338)
*Maoyang Xiang,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAPA是一种针对Transformer架构的硬件友好型激活函数，通过利用预激活数据的分布特性，采用非均匀分段近似方法，在保持性能的同时显著提升硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性激活函数在设备端推理和训练中消耗大量硬件资源，严重影响系统性能和能效。现有方法在硬件效率和性能之间存在权衡，需要一种既能保持模型性能又能优化硬件实现的激活函数。

Method: 提出分布感知分段激活(DAPA)：1) 利用预激活数据分布进行非均匀分段近似，在高概率区域分配更细的分段；2) 使用分布加权均方误差进行量化，减少硬件部署的延迟和资源使用；3) 设计硬件友好的实现方案。

Result: HLS实现显示：DAPA将GELU计算速度提升16倍，DSP利用率降低16倍。在视觉Transformer和GPT-2模型上保持相当或更好的性能表现。

Conclusion: DAPA是一种可微分、硬件友好的激活函数，通过分布感知的非均匀分段近似和量化策略，在保持Transformer模型性能的同时显著提升硬件效率，适用于设备端部署。

Abstract: Non-linear activation functions play a pivotal role in on-device inference and training, as they not only consume substantial hardware resources but also impose a significant impact on system performance and energy efficiency. In this work, we propose Distribution-Aware Piecewise Activation (DAPA), a differentiable and hardware-friendly activation function for Transformer architectures by exploiting the distribution of pre-activation data. DAPA employs a non-uniform piecewise approximation that allocates finer segments to high-probability regions of the distribution, improving generalizability over prior piecewise linear methods. The resulting approximation is further quantized using Distribution-Weighted Mean Square Error to reduce latency and resource utilization for hardware deployment. Our HLS implementation demonstrates that DAPA speeds up GELU computation by 16$\times$ and decreases DSP utilization by 16$\times$ while maintaining comparable or better performance across vision Transformers and GPT-2 models.

</details>


### [151] [Anatomical Heterogeneity in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19348)
*Tomasz Wietrzykowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer语言模型存在显著的层间异质性，挑战了传统均匀计算预算训练的假设，提出了基于层重要性的差异化训练策略，可大幅降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer语言模型采用均匀计算预算训练所有层，隐含假设层间同质性。本研究通过实证分析挑战这一假设，探索层间解剖学异质性及其对训练效率的影响。

Method: 使用SmolLM2-135M模型（30层，1.35亿参数），采用五种诊断指标：权重可预测性（R2）、消融退化、恢复速度、权重操作鲁棒性和结构分析，系统分析层间异质性。

Result: 发现显著的解剖学异质性：1）层权重具有强数学规律性但非线性误差累积导致预测权重灾难性失败；2）层重要性跨越10^7范围，存在关键核心层和反层；3）恢复速度与层重要性相关；4）仅权重缩放能保持模型质量；5）基于层重要性的差异化训练可降低54%成本。

Conclusion: Transformer层间存在显著异质性，均匀训练假设不成立。基于层重要性的差异化训练策略（Growth Transformer Training）能大幅提高训练效率，为高效语言模型训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Current transformer language models are trained with uniform computational budgets across all layers, implicitly assuming layer homogeneity. We challenge this assumption through empirical analysis of SmolLM2-135M, a 30-layer, 135M-parameter causal language model, using five diagnostic metrics: weight predictability (R2), ablation degradation, recovery speed, weight manipulation robustness, and structural analysis. We find profound anatomical heterogeneity: (1) Layer weights follow strong mathematical regularity (R2 = 0.91) with a universal oscillatory delta pattern (correlation ~= -0.50), yet predicted weights cause catastrophic failure due to nonlinear error accumulation. (2) Layer importance spans a 10^7 range, from a critical core (L8-11, up to +63,419% PPL degradation) to anti-layers (L14, L17) whose removal improves performance. (3) Recovery speed correlates with layer importance, indicating differential training requirements. (4) Only weight scaling (alpha = 0.9) preserves model quality among five tested manipulation strategies. (5) Growth Transformer Training, allocating budget by layer importance, achieves ~54% cost reduction. A proof-of-concept experiment confirms this: 4.7x lower validation loss than uniform training at identical parameter count, while being 13% faster.

</details>


### [152] [GoAgent: Group-of-Agents Communication Topology Generation for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19677)
*Hongjiang Chen,Xin Zheng,Yixin Liu,Pengfei Jiao,Shiyuan Li,Huan Liu,Zhidong Zhao,Ziqi Xu,Ibrahim Khalil,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GoAgent提出了一种基于群组的通信拓扑生成方法，将协作群组作为多智能体系统构建的基本单元，通过条件信息瓶颈压缩通信冗余，在六个基准测试中达到93.84%平均准确率并减少约17%的令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统大多采用节点中心化的通信拓扑生成方式，群组结构仅从局部连接决策中隐式出现，导致协调效率低下和通信开销过大。需要显式建模协作群组作为系统构建的基本单元。

Method: GoAgent首先通过LLM枚举任务相关的候选群组，然后自回归地选择和连接这些群组作为原子单元来构建最终通信图，同时捕获群组内聚性和群组间协调性。引入条件信息瓶颈目标来压缩群组间通信，保留任务相关信号同时过滤冗余历史噪声。

Result: 在六个基准测试中实现了93.84%的平均准确率，达到最先进性能，同时减少了约17%的令牌消耗，有效降低了通信冗余和噪声传播。

Conclusion: 将协作群组作为多智能体系统构建的原子单元，并采用条件信息瓶颈压缩通信，能够显著提升系统性能同时降低通信开销，为LLM-based MAS的通信拓扑设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in solving complex tasks, yet their effectiveness depends heavily on the underlying communication topology that coordinates agent interactions. Within these systems, successful problem-solving often necessitates task-specific group structures to divide and conquer subtasks. However, most existing approaches generate communication topologies in a node-centric manner, leaving group structures to emerge implicitly from local connectivity decisions rather than modeling them explicitly, often leading to suboptimal coordination and unnecessary communication overhead. To address this limitation, we propose GoAgent (Group-of-Agents), a communication topology generation method that explicitly treats collaborative groups as the atomic units of MAS construction. Specifically, GoAgent first enumerates task-relevant candidate groups through an LLM and then autoregressively selects and connects these groups as atomic units to construct the final communication graph, jointly capturing intra-group cohesion and inter-group coordination. To mitigate communication redundancy and noise propagation inherent in expanding topologies, we further introduce a conditional information bottleneck (CIB) objective that compresses inter-group communication, preserving task-relevant signals while filtering out redundant historical noise. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of GoAgent with 93.84% average accuracy while reducing token consumption by about 17%.

</details>


### [153] [A Mathematical Theory of Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19349)
*Bahar Taşkesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个数学模型，分析学习者知识结构对信息吸收的瓶颈效应，揭示教学速度受限于先决条件可达性和目标不确定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI降低了信息生产成本，但信息的价值取决于下游用户能否吸收和运用。信号只对具有相应解码结构能力的学习者才有意义，学习者侧的知识结构瓶颈限制了教学效果

Method: 建立数学模型，将学习者抽象为具有概念先决结构的心智系统，教学建模为与潜在目标的顺序通信。有效通信信道取决于学习者当前知识状态，随学习进展变得更富信息量

Result: 模型揭示学习速度的两个限制：由先决条件可达性决定的结构限制，以及由目标不确定性决定的认知限制。框架显示训练和能力获取存在阈值效应，当教学视野低于目标先决深度时，额外教学无法成功；一旦达到该深度，完成变得可行。在异质学习者中，通用广播课程比个性化教学慢线性倍数

Conclusion: 学习者知识结构是信息吸收的关键瓶颈，教学有效性取决于学习者解码信号的结构能力。模型为理解AI时代教学和学习过程提供了形式化框架，强调个性化教学的重要性

Abstract: Generative AI has transformed the economics of information production, making explanations, proofs, examples, and analyses available at very low cost. Yet the value of information still depends on whether downstream users can absorb and act on it. A signal conveys meaning only to a learner with the structural capacity to decode it: an explanation that clarifies a concept for one user may be indistinguishable from noise to another who lacks the relevant prerequisites. This paper develops a mathematical model of that learner-side bottleneck. We model the learner as a mind, an abstract learning system characterized by a prerequisite structure over concepts. A mind may represent a human learner, an artificial learner such as a neural network, or any agent whose ability to interpret signals depends on previously acquired concepts. Teaching is modeled as sequential communication with a latent target. Because instructional signals are usable only when the learner has acquired the prerequisites needed to parse them, the effective communication channel depends on the learner's current state of knowledge and becomes more informative as learning progresses. The model yields two limits on the speed of learning and adoption: a structural limit determined by prerequisite reachability and an epistemic limit determined by uncertainty about the target. The framework implies threshold effects in training and capability acquisition. When the teaching horizon lies below the prerequisite depth of the target, additional instruction cannot produce successful completion of teaching; once that depth is reached, completion becomes feasible. Across heterogeneous learners, a common broadcast curriculum can be slower than personalized instruction by a factor linear in the number of learner types.

</details>


### [154] [FedRG: Unleashing the Representation Geometry for Federated Learning with Noisy Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19722)
*Tian Wen,Zhiqin Yang,Yonggang Zhang,Xuefeng Jiang,Hao Peng,Yuwei Wang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedRG：一种基于表示几何优先原则的联邦学习噪声标签处理方法，通过自监督创建标签无关的球面表示，利用vMF混合模型识别噪声样本，实现异构场景下的鲁棒优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在分布式场景中面临噪声标注导致的性能下降问题。现有方法依赖标量损失值识别噪声样本，但在异构场景下缺乏可靠性。需要从表示几何的角度重新思考噪声标签识别范式。
